# Compile audit: improvements-r38

## AUDIT-1
- auditor: fresh isolated compile reviewer, no compiler context
- audited_at: 2026-09-02
- scope: ir_sha256=5eb2f0344ef6465efc37fa57c40c1bb4a878bfcc4f733fcf06f14acae71de6a5 — Verified the IR bytes (sha matches) and the binding of all 30 sources (manifest file sha256 = on-disk bytes for 30/30; the IR's source_sha256 is sha256 of the transcript body from "## Meddelande 1", stripped, and matches 30/30; inventory_revision 38 / inventory_sha256 match the manifest; message counts match 30/30; validate = 0 FAIL 0 WARN). Wrote my own parser and checker: 174 items, 738 provenance entries all resolve to existing turns; 173/173 quotes are exact whitespace-normalised substrings of their cited ranges; all 70 OWNER_DECISIONs cite an owner turn or an owner-answered RQ and every one of their quotes sits inside an owner turn or RQ owner_answer. Extracted and read all 581 owner turns of all 30 sources myself (pasted agent/terminal output condensed), read every OWNER_DECISION with its cited context for ~25 items directly, and used 7 sub-agents: two adversarial fidelity checks over 32 items across all seven kinds (21 faithful, 11 minor drift, 0 material infidelity, no commitment language in OPTION/HYPOTHESIS items), one full read of the 11 small/watch-only sources (all substantively used except three token citations noted below), and four second-pass sweeps over every owner turn of the remaining 19 sources with role checks on all 70 owner decisions. Grep-verified 60+ named constructs in claims against the corpus (all present; "build bottom-up, sell top-down" = CONV-030 msg 5 "Bygg nedifrån och upp — sälj uppifrån och ned"). Falsification attempts that resolved in the compile's favour: RQ-027 concepts never appear as OWNER_DECISION (Attention Compiler / Experience-to-Capability Compiler absent; Role Shell and Quiet Organization Contract only in OPTION/DERIVED_JUDGMENT) and RQ-028 exemplars sit only in RND-054/067 (DERIVED/UNKNOWN) with no OWNER_DECISION citing CONV-007 msgs 102–117; RND-091's "verbatim" chain adoption holds (CONV-001 msg 116 restates msg 111 line for line); RND-073's adoption holds via msg 48 "Kan du ge mig tilläggen och den stora tillsammans?"; RND-010's INBOX rule was issued by the owner (template authorship untraceable); the knowledge-repo vs "detta ska till innovation i nuläget" pair is a narrowing, both items present and narrated in RND-082 — not a silently resolved contradiction; the executor swings are all present as items (RND-029/030/031/032; only 031→030 typed supersedes, ordering derivable from source); the three WELL_EXPLORED lenses are genuinely explored in their basis (tested-in-reality events and owner decisions) and no lens state is overstated; item order is thematic, no priority/roadmap/lifecycle prose beyond source-quoted laws; no invented kinds (only oddity: RND-116 carries "supersedes" as a tag with no superseded item); the late deep R&D (CONV-028/029/030 cited by 36/9/15 items) is typed HYPOTHESIS/OPTION/DERIVED_JUDGMENT with explicit uncertainty and execution events are OBSERVATION with execution-event tags. What did not survive is below: the compile applies its own "ge mig prompt = adoption" rule to Intake items but not to owner-attention/delegation mechanisms, drops three single-turn owner decisions while carrying the surrounding execution volume, and states one false consequence in an OWNER_DECISION.
- verdict: FINDINGS

### FIND-001
- finding: RND_SECOND_TRUTH
- severity: material
- evidence: RND-045, RND-048, RND-053, RND-055 and lens executive-function (note). CONV-003 msg 12 answers msg 11 (the surgical delegation patch CONTINUE / ATTENTION_CONTINUE / ROUTE / HARD_STOP, "jag hade gjort den här korrigeringen direkt nu") with an unambiguous prompt request, and CONV-015 msgs 129 + 131 answer msg 128 ("göra den typen av mekaniskt begränsad prerequisite-migration till stående delegerad authority") likewise — the same speech act the compile counts as owner adoption for RND-005 (CONV-013 msg 206), RND-092, RND-098 and RND-099. The IR instead states in RND-045 "the mechanism was never owner-chosen", in RND-053 "the mechanism was never owner-chosen", in RND-055 "none owner-selected", records CONV-015 msg 128 in RND-048 only as "proposed widening the delegation", and writes in the executive-function note "every attention mechanism is an unadopted proposal". Only execution of the two prompts is unevidenced; the owner choice is in the cited ranges (RND-045 cites msg 3-13; RND-048 cites 126-132). The sweep's own package (owner-attention-inte-owner-stop brief) records the patch as "commissioned by the owner (← msg 12)".
- quote: "ge mig prompten för det" / "jag har redan skickat den andra prompten så jag vill bara ha det som löser framtida onödiga ägarstopp"

### FIND-002
- finding: RND_SECOND_TRUTH
- severity: material
- evidence: CONV-008 msg 29 is an owner-worded correction that changed the bootstrap's path: the assistant conceded (msg 32 "du har helt rätt att reagera" … "Min tidigare formulering att H-032:s produkt “aldrig byggdes” var för stark" and set the rule "Treat absence from main as “not promoted/finalized”, not “never implemented”"), rewrote the master continuation (msg 35 "This prompt supersedes any earlier instruction that described H-032 as “never built”"), and Claude Code credited it (msg 36 "Following your correction, I reconstructed the full history and found the R122–R128 product was fully implemented"). No item cites msg 29 as owner voice (RND-025 and RND-039 only sweep it inside execution ranges; RND-025 says "the recovered H-032 product" without attribution) and the resulting operating rule is absent, while RND-033 asserts "option chooser by proxy (the assistant chose on every fork)" and "The owner never articulated this role in own words".
- quote: "Hur kan det stämma? Codex vet jag har hållt på mycket med h032?"

### FIND-010
- finding: RND_FREQUENCY_BIAS
- severity: material
- evidence: RND-007 (and the 10 other `convergence`-tagged items RND-018, RND-038, RND-053, RND-072, RND-075, RND-077, RND-103, RND-123, RND-125, RND-150; 22 items in all assert cross-thread recurrence, several as counted strength — "across five threads", "in seven threads", "in eight threads", "in nine threads", "repeated in six threads"). The bound corpus is one ChatGPT project (`project-manifest.json` origin `…/improvements/project`, IR `source_set.kind=project`) with cross-chat memory and shared project files: 406 `memcite` markers in 28 of 30 sources, 977 `filecite` markers in 23 of 30. The corpus itself already found and repaired this exact error on the same material: an independent semantic review of the earlier cross-chat synthesis of these briefs required (CONV-016 msg 46) that the shared-context channel be added as an explicit bias factor — its four named items are the same ChatGPT project ("Improvements"), shared project files/filecite context, cross-chat memory/memcite and explicit cross-chat references — that the grades INDEPENDENT_CONVERGENCE / DEPENDENT_REFINEMENT / SHARED_PRIOR_CONTEXT / UNKNOWN be preserved, and that inflated chat counts be corrected; the owner ran it (msg 47, `Grupp 1 — Shared prior context / konvergensspråk: FIXED`) and froze it (msg 53, `SEMANTIC_REMEDIATION=4/4_FIXED`, `PHASE_2_FROZEN=YES`). The IR carries none of it: zero occurrences of "shared prior", "shared context", "cross-chat memory", "memcite", "same project", "bias factor", "SHARED_PRIOR_CONTEXT", "DEPENDENT_REFINEMENT" or "INDEPENDENT_CONVERGENCE". RND-007 goes further and asserts independence outright, as does RND-085 ("Assistant diagnoses, twice and independently"), and RND-007's own thread count is wrong in the direction the review corrected — it says "across five threads" while its provenance spans seven conversations (CONV-001, CONV-005, CONV-006, CONV-010, CONV-012, CONV-022, CONV-030). The compile therefore renders `FREQUENCY_IS_IMPORTANCE=NO` and recites "frequency ≠ importance" inside RND-103 while its convergence layer rests on unqualified thread counts in a shared-memory channel.
- quote: "Do NOT call the five-chat compile pattern" / "The rule recurs independently in each thread with the same shape."

### FIND-011
- finding: RND_SECOND_TRUTH
- severity: material
- evidence: RND-053 and lens executive-function (note). The remediation of FIND-001 replaced "never owner-chosen" with a count that the compile's own items falsify. RND-053 now states the owner "commissioned two mechanisms by prompt request" — naming CONV-003 msg 12 and CONV-015 msgs 129/131 — "without re-wording any boundary himself", immediately after listing "hard-stop list, delegation classes, shadow mode, per-step go" as the boundaries that are assistant- or agent-authored; the executive-function note repeats "the owner commissioned two by prompt request". But RND-049 (OWNER_DECISION, CONV-013 msgs 172–174, `supports→RND-053`) already records a third: "The owner granted Claude a standing autonomous mandate … the assistant drafted it as: no per-slice stops, guarded publication authorized, seven STOP conditions only", and RND-050 records a fourth ("The assistant-authored prompts the owner adopted en bloc bound this with manual-SMS retention, progressive autonomy and shadow mode"). A fifth is carried nowhere: at CONV-016 msg 94 the owner commissioned the prompt whose referent (msgs 91/93) is unambiguous, and msg 95 delivers the hard-stop taxonomy itself — `# HUMAN HARD STOPS … Stop only for a real condition such as: HIGHER_AUTHORITY_CONTRADICTION / NEW_MATERIAL_AUTHORITY_EXPANSION / …` and `NORMAL_WORKFLOW_TRANSITION ≠ HUMAN_STOP` — which RND-054 cites as assistant-authored while RND-030 (CONV-016 msgs 92–95) records only the executor allocation and RND-047, demoted this round, now reads "a direction, typed as owner-voiced observation rather than a content decision". The same speech act the compile counts as adoption for RND-005, RND-092, RND-175, RND-176 and RND-179 is uncounted here, and the number "two" is false on the compile's own evidence. The rule is applied unevenly outside this lens too: CONV-019 msgs 11 and 16 ("Gör en fullständig skiss utifrån detta") are the owner direction and commission that produced msg 19, and no item cites either, while RND-123 — which cites msg 19 — lists the owner's backing as "owner-backed only on direction (CONV-005 msg 28; CONV-015 msgs 83, 157)".
- quote: "commissioned two mechanisms by prompt request" / "uppdatera prompten, ge mig den fulla"

### FIND-012
- finding: RND_SECOND_TRUTH
- severity: material
- evidence: RND-035 (OWNER_DECISION, CONV-015 msgs 258–264). Its claim states as accomplished fact that "a new fixture regime on the Mac was cut instead". Inside its own cited range the assistant's consequence block (msg 264) says the opposite — `NEW_PORTABLE_FIXTURE_REGIME_REQUIRED=YES`, i.e. required, not performed — and the rest of the same source proves the cut never happened: owner turns 281, 288, 293, 297, 302, 307, 317, 322 and 327 all report `FOUNDATION_FIXTURE_STATUS=KANDIDAT_EJ_KLIPPT` / `NY_REGIM_KLIPPT=false untouched; no §A6 cut`, and the assistant's prompts through msg 336 repeat `Do NOT flip NY_REGIM_KLIPPT`. CONV-013 confirms it after the Foundation smoke cut did occur: msg 94 records `FOUNDATION_FIXTURE_STATUS=KLIPPT 2026-08-25` together with `NY_REGIM_KLIPPT=false` and `UNIVERSAL_REGIME_REMAINS_UNCUT=YES`. RND-035's uncertainty ("none — verbatim owner; consequence text (FIXTURE_REGIME_CHANGE) is assistant-drafted") does not disclose that the transition is unperformed, so the derived layer reports a completed trust transition that the repository state in the source says did not happen — the failure mode RND-001's own law exists to prevent.
- quote: "a new fixture regime on the Mac was cut instead" / "NEW_PORTABLE_FIXTURE_REGIME_REQUIRED=YES"

### FIND-013
- finding: RND_RECENCY_BIAS
- severity: material
- evidence: RND-073 and RND-166, both OWNER_DECISION with owner provenance, hold two different versions of the same owner-adopted sequence and carry no relation to each other (RND-073 RELS: depends-on→RND-072, relates-to→RND-074; RND-166 RELS: supports→RND-167, relates-to→RND-148). RND-073 records "digital förvaltning → bootstrap checkpoint → Autonomy Kernel v1 → architecture inventory → Organization OS V0 → Verkstadsgolvet projection" (CONV-005 msgs 42–48). At CONV-008 msg 179 the owner himself marks that chain as prior — "Tidigare pratade vi om den här planeringen: FULL AUTONOMY BOOTSTRAP ↓ AUTONOMY KERNEL v1 ↓ … ↓ Organization OS V0 ↓ VERKSTADSGOLVET DIGITAL TWIN" — the assistant answers with a "Reviderad huvudroadmap" inserting Owner Plane V0 → Owner Console V0 → Typed Actuation V0 before the Digital Twin (msg 185), and the owner adopts it at msgs 183 and 186. RND-166's own claim calls it "the revised roadmap". Neither `supersedes` nor `contradicts` links the pair, so a consumer reading RND-073 gets an owner-decided ordering the owner superseded in his own words, with uncertainty "low".
- quote: "Tidigare pratade vi om den här planeringen" / "Detta låter bra, låt oss planera för detta."

### FIND-014
- finding: RND_OWNER_PROVENANCE_LAUNDERED
- severity: minor

The same convention fails in the other direction at RND-031, an OWNER_DECISION whose entire owner provenance is CONV-016 msg 108 — a want plus the question `Kan vi återgå till Codex jobba klart eller hur tänker vi`, which the assistant answers at msg 112 with `jag tycker vi ska växla tillbaka och låta Codex bli primär bootstrap-agent nu` — a question the restated convention assigns to owner-voiced OBSERVATION, and RND-031 nevertheless carries the `supersedes` edge over RND-030 that the contract reserves for an OWNER_DECISION with owner provenance; (b) RND-066 now reads "dramatically more autonomous than it did", "inte bara modellen", and carries the assistant's "möjlig äkta Codex-fördel". **Ruling on the disputed FIND-020 term: AUDIT-3 was wrong and the compiler is right.** A role-partitioned grep over all 30 sources finds `tilitsbaserad` (one l) in exactly one turn in the corpus — CONV-005 msg 13, `Johnny (användare)`, inside RND-069's own cited range 13-15 — one turn *before* the assistant's first `tillitsbaserad` (msg 14); AUDIT-3's grep for the two-l spelling missed the owner's own spelling. The compiler's reassignments are also right and I verified each: `delegationsordning` occurs only in assistant turns (CONV-003 msg 7, CONV-005 msg 14, CONV-021 msg 2), `APT` as a word only in assistant turns (CONV-005 msgs 22, 29, 41, 49, 51, 57) while the owner wrote `arbetsplatsträffar` (msg 21), and `Competence Ledger` (msg 14) and `Service Fabric` (msg 33) are assistant coinages inside RND-069's cited ranges. The correction does not over-correct: every owner term the new claim lists is verbatim in an owner turn in range (msg 13 tilitsbaserad ledning och styrning, 19 avvikelsehantering, 21 arbetsplatsträffar, 25 årshjul, 32 verksamhetsstöd, 34 hemteam), the list is explicitly non-exhaustive ("terms such as"), and the owner in fact supplies more than it names (kommundirektör/enhetschef/verksamhetschef/förvaltningschef msg 15, medarbetarsamtal/prestationsamtal msg 25, lokalvårdare/chefsstöd msg 32, professioner msg 34), so the owner is not under-credited; the only over-reach is calling the standard SKR abbreviation `APT` the assistant's "coinage" when the accurate claim is that the assistant introduced it, which is stylistic. What the correction did *not* do is fix the second half of FIND-020, RND-076, raised below as FIND-026. ### FIND-024
- finding: RND_OWNER_PROVENANCE_LAUNDERED
- severity: material
- evidence: RND-107, RND-108, RND-109, RND-110 and lens rnd-intake (note). All four are OWNER_DECISION with `authority_class: owner`, `uncertainty: none — owner-answered review-queue entry`, and RQ-023/024/025/026 as their only provenance — but the decisive wording of each is assistant-authored, and no claim says so. RQ-026's `owner_answer` is byte-identical (492 characters, whitespace-normalised) to the text the ASSISTANT dictates at CONV-001 msg 92: "Skriv detta:" followed by exactly that paragraph, and RND-110's quote is its closing sentence. RQ-023's core is verbatim from assistant msg 86 ("Hanteras med ägardelta när respektive paket aktiveras"), and RQ-024's and RQ-025's cores are the option labels of Claude Code menu screens that sit inside owner turns 87 and 89 — pasted UI, which the same compile treats as external evidence everywhere else. The owner turns 85/87/89/91 contain no prose of his own at all: they are checkbox screens (`☒ Reversals ☒ Assist-only ☐ Relationer ✔ Submit`) and option lists. The owner act is real (he submitted each group), but this is adoption en bloc of assistant-authored text, and the compile's own uniform standard for that is disclosure — RND-005 "the boundary content is assistant-authored", RND-099 "the 'OWNER AUTHORITY' text … is assistant-authored prompt text", RND-179 "the requirement text is assistant-authored", RND-182 "all named assistant-authored", RND-184 "the review design and their findings are assistant- and Claude-authored". These four alone carry no such sentence and no residual uncertainty, and the rnd-intake note counts them as "four owner-answered review-queue entries" backing the corpus's most owner-decided lens.
- quote: "Skriv detta: > **För denna proving-run: stäng RQ-022 och defer bilagehydrering. … Saknad lastbärande bilaga ska redovisas som known source gap, inte döljas som komplett corpus.**" / "none — owner-answered review-queue entry"

### FIND-025
- finding: RND_SECOND_TRUTH
- severity: material
- evidence: RND-185 (new this round). Its claim enumerates what the eight cited commissions authorized as "R7 through R11, the pre-cut reconciliation, the §A6 cut-compatibility work and the independent gate adjudication", and the enumeration is wrong at both ends. R7 was already published and closed before the first cited turn: CONV-013 msg 29 reads `R7_PUBLISHED=YES … R8_BLOCKED_BY_R7=NO` and "**R8 får nu starta från exakt `b94718be…`**", and msg 31 answers msg 30 with "Jag tar fram **R8-prompten**" — the eight commissions authorize R8, R9, R10, R11, the pre-cut reconciliation (msg 64), the §A6 CUT itself (msg 74), the gate adjudication (msg 88) and the Foundation GREEN attestation / Step-0 closeout (msg 95), the last of which the claim never names. Worse, the msg-74 commission is not "cut-compatibility work" — the compatibility/authority verification is msg 64's, already counted separately as "the pre-cut reconciliation". Msg 73 ends "**Nu är vi faktiskt framme vid själva mänskliga HÖGRISK §A6 Foundation CUT-ceremonin.** Nästa prompt ska …" and msg 75 answers msg 74 with "Jag tar fram **själva CUT-prompten** … så prompten blir en riktig HÖGRISK trust transition", the transition msg 65 calls "en **irreversibel trust transition**"; the owner then pastes `PR #123 öppnad — klippet är utfört` at msg 83. So the one commission in the whole corpus that authorized an irreversible human HÖGRISK trust transition is renamed as compatibility work and swept into the item's own exemption ("they authorize the next step of an already-running arc rather than introducing a new mechanism"), which is the ground on which it is typed OBSERVATION rather than OWNER_DECISION.
- quote: "the owner authorized R7 through R11, the pre-cut reconciliation, the §A6 cut-compatibility work and the independent gate adjudication" / "R7_PUBLISHED=YES" / "Nu är vi faktiskt framme vid själva mänskliga HÖGRISK §A6 Foundation CUT-ceremonin."

### FIND-028
- finding: RND_SECOND_TRUTH
- severity: material
- evidence: RND-033 (OBSERVATION, CONV-008 msgs 29, 36-46, 75-95, 109-124; CONV-015 msgs 234-336; CONV-013 msgs 25-81). Its claim makes the owner "the only actor able to reboot the host, produce bare-host evidence and push/merge". The reboot and bare-host halves hold (CONV-008 msg 36 "Please reboot the machine"; msg 46 "i en vanlig macOS Terminal utanför Claude Code"), but the push/merge exclusivity is falsified twice inside the item's own cited ranges, both times by the assistant acting on the repository through its GitHub connector: CONV-015 msg 258 (`ChatGPT (assistent)`, inside 234-336) states "Jag har också redan gjort en konkret owner-action: **`nortropic-system #102` är mergad** till `main`, merge-SHA `31c6ef3faf5bf217889f8267ab522db98b751ad4`" and the owner's next turn (msg 259) does not contest it; CONV-013 msg 81 (`ChatGPT (assistent)`, the last message of the cited 25-81) states "Jag publicerar nu CUT-compatibility som **normal merge commit**, guarded mot exakt head `839ef8ac…`". The same connector capability is independently evidenced at CONV-023 msgs 17-18, where the assistant creates issue #4 in `Nortropic/innovation-intake`. The item's uncertainty ("low as observation of the material") discloses no doubt about the exclusivity, and the clause inflates the owner's operational agency in the one item whose subject is that agency — which RND-034 and the executive-function lens then build on.

### FIND-030
- finding: RND_NEGATIVE_SPACE_OMITTED
- severity: material
- evidence: CONV-013 msgs 128–153 — the plan-recovery episode — is absent from all 188 items. Of those 26 turns only two are cited anywhere, and both only as frustration quotes in RND-156 (msgs 133 and 146). What is dropped is an owner decision and its consequence: at msg 128 the owner originates the model the compile later credits to the msg-159/181 commissions ("planen ligger väl i nortropic intake? kan vi inte peka mot den och jämföra vars vi är"); at msgs 143/146 he states the constraint that the two uploaded masterplan files cannot be downloaded; the assistant concedes at msg 149 that File Library gives it "referenser/innehåll, inte råa filbytes"; at msg 150 the owner authorizes producing a substitute and putting it back; and msgs 152–153 deliver a "source-bound recovered masterplan" the assistant itself marks as not byte-identical with the originals. The strings "recovered masterplan", "byte-identical" and "File Library" have zero occurrences in the IR. The compile therefore narrates loss → law (RND-039 "an approved Webbförvaltningen plan vanished in compaction"; RND-038 "The law was written after a real loss"; RND-096 the commissioned fail-closed plan contract) while omitting the one owner act in the corpus that runs against that law — an owner-authorized model reconstruction standing in for a lost plan authority — and the continuity lens note claims "real losses, real recoveries" without it. RND-039 cites msgs 154-158, which carry only the post-hoc diagnosis; RND-096 starts at msg 154.
- quote: "Gör det så vi kan lägga in det igen och inventera vars vi är" / "eftersom File Library inte ger mig råbytes är den samlade filen **inte byte-identisk med originalen**"

- finding: RND_SECOND_TRUTH

(a) It calls CONV-013 msg 159 "the first commission the compile credits for it", but RND-190 — added the previous round and linked from RND-189 by `relates-to` — is an OWNER_DECISION crediting the owner commission at CONV-013 msg 150, "Gör det så vi kan lägga in det igen och inventera vars vi är", whose delivered artifact at msg 153 is precisely the inventory prompt RND-189 names as one of its two implementations, printing `MASTERPLAN / ↓ / CURRENT nortropic-system / ↓ / IMPLEMENTED / PARTIAL / NOT STARTED / DEFERRED`. The distance from msg 128 to the first credited commission is therefore 22 messages, not thirty-one. (b) The claim ends "the owner voices the same comparison in two other threads" and the uncertainty names CONV-011 msg 5 and CONV-019 msg 20. CONV-011 msg 5 qualifies (that thread's comparison is explicit at msg 3, "gå igenom hela planen 1 och 1 och jämför mot nuvarande"). CONV-019 msg 20 does not: it voices an inventory of the current state with no stored plan to compare against, the assistant's answer at msg 21 calls step one "att mäta vad Nortropic faktiskt är idag", and the compile's own RND-178 types that turn as sequencing. The clause is supported by one thread, not two.
- quote: "thirty-one messages before the first commission the compile credits for it (msg 159)" / "the owner voices the same comparison in two other threads" / "Gör det så vi kan lägga in det igen och inventera vars vi är"

### FIND-037
- finding: RND_SECOND_TRUTH

DIGITAL BODY OF KNOWLEDGE"), and the tenth item in RND-139's list — "a Digital Director that may output DO_NOTHING_NOW" — is not among them: the Digital Director belongs to the earlier Digital Department gap set at CONV-015 msg 225 and DO_NOTHING_NOW is that same prompt's separate "STRATEGIC INACTION" section. The compile itself gets this right elsewhere: RND-138 places "a Digital Director" inside the gap set and counts the layers separately as "nine cross-cutting operating-system layers".
- quote: "The nine cross-cutting layers the owner adopted by prompt request" / "a Digital Director that may output DO_NOTHING_NOW" / "The answer requires nine final cross-cutting capabilities:"

### FIND-038
- finding: RND_OWNER_PROVENANCE_LAUNDERED
- severity: material
- evidence: RND-188 is billed as "The one commission in the corpus that authorized an irreversible human HÖGRISK trust transition" and rests on CONV-013 msg 74 ("ge mig prompten"), with `uncertainty: none for the commission (referent unambiguous at msg 73)`. The bytes falsify the middle of the claim. Msg 73 (assistant) does end "Nu är vi faktiskt framme vid själva mänskliga HÖGRISK §A6 Foundation CUT-ceremonin.", and msg 74 is the owner's commission — but the prompt delivered on it, at msg 77, is headed `NO CUT IN THIS ASSIGNMENT` and expects "no cut commit no gate verdict"; msg 77 opens by withdrawing the cut. The owner then pastes the "FOUNDATION CUT COMPATIBILITY STOP REPORT … No cut." at msg 78, and msgs 79, 80, 81 and 82 are all assistant turns — the actual cut prompt is issued at the end of msg 82 ("Det här är nu **själva HÖGRISK §A6 Foundation CUT-prompten**") with **no owner turn between msg 78 and msg 82**. RND-188's provenance is CONV-013:73-75 and 82-83, skipping exactly the msgs 76–81 where the redirection and the intervening round happen. RND-185 compounds it: its eight commissions (msgs 30, 38, 46, 55, 64, 74, 88, 95) are mapped onto nine rounds including "the §A6 cut itself", omitting the cut-compatibility round the corpus's own closure names ("R1–R11, pre-cut-reconciliation, cut-compatibility och §A6-klippet", msg 94). The single irreversible trust transition in the corpus is therefore credited to an owner commission that produced an explicitly non-cutting round.
- quote: "the assistant said they had reached the §A6 Foundation CUT ceremony itself and the owner asked for that prompt; the delivered prompt performed the cut as one HÖGRISK commit and stopped before merge" / "Jag hittade **en sista mekanisk blocker innan själva cutten**, och den är viktig nog att vi inte ska klippa ännu." / "NO CUT IN THIS ASSIGNMENT"

### FIND-039
- finding: RND_OWNER_PROVENANCE_LAUNDERED
- severity: material
- evidence: RND-157 (OWNER_DECISION) states that the owner "liked Munder Difflin's UX while explicitly excluding its avatars and Scrum board ('jag gillade uxen men gubbarna, scrum')". The owner's cited words are an elliptical fragment naming two things after "men"; they state no verdict. The item's own cited ranges refute the exclusion: CONV-005 msg 73, inside its `CONV-005:69-73` range, has the owner naming Gathertown "med gubbarna" as a positive model, and CONV-012 msgs 1–5, inside its `CONV-012:1-5` range, dedicate the whole Aquarium chat to "arbetsmyror/avatarer". Two turns later the owner asks for exactly what is said to be excluded, and the compile carries that in a different OWNER_DECISION — RND-154's "workers as a small ant colony" at CONV-005:76-77 — with no relation to RND-157. The in-thread assistant read the same fragment the other way at msg 4 ("Jag tror därför att "gubbarna" definitivt ska in"). Separately, RND-157's "excluding … Scrum board" and RND-183's "The owner named a method he wanted — Scrum" stand in the compile with no `contradicts` or `relates-to` between them. A reader is told the owner rejected avatars when his settled position is avatars bound to proven state.
- quote: "liked Munder Difflin's UX while explicitly excluding its avatars and Scrum board ('jag gillade uxen men gubbarna, scrum')" / "Det blir våran egen ant colony, små arbetsmyror" / "Jag tror därför att “gubbarna” definitivt ska in"

### FIND-040

In 191 items `PARALLEL_SAFE` and `SERIAL_TRUST_TRANSITION` occur zero times; `DEPENDENCY_BLOCKED` occurs once, in RND-046, as an unexplained fragment of the overnight mandate. The coordinator boundary that closes it at CONV-016 msg 124 is likewise absent, and CONV-016 msgs 118–124 are cited by no item at all (RND-149 cites only 126–128). RND-180's one clause "parallel discovery, falsification and verification with serial authority", from CONV-015:26-28 alone, is all that survives of it, and neither the executive-function nor the continuity-operate-forever lens basis carries the rule. A compile that enumerates far more marginal named constructs leaves the autonomy kernel's own concurrency law unnamed and unscoped.
- quote: "Maximize PARALLEL_SAFE work. Never parallelize SERIAL_TRUST_TRANSITION operations merely for speed." / "bara coordinatorn får driva **kanonisk gate-freeze, kandidatval, publication, owner-live, supervisor-resume och checkpoint**"

This is the same class as FIND-038/039 (an owner question read as an owner verdict); the item's core at msgs 126–132 is verbatim-backed and unaffected, which is why this is minor.

### FIND-045
- finding: RND_AUTHORITY_LAUNDERING
- severity: minor
- evidence: RND-192 (OBSERVATION, authority_class evidence) closes its claim "It recurs across three threads and the owner relays the classification back in his own message", and its uncertainty says "the owner's own use is a relay of the classification for one substep (CONV-015 msg 19)". CONV-015 msg 19 is the item's only cited owner turn — the other 11 provenance entries (CONV-008 8/35/98, CONV-015 28/30/33/58/145, CONV-016 120/122/124) are all `ChatGPT (assistent)`, which I verified by role parse. That turn is 4,918 characters of pure pasted Codex output with zero owner-authored words: it opens directly on Codex's own classification block and continues in Codex's first person ("Jag rättar nu endast de tre senast bevisade gatebristerna … Ingen produktkod berörs"), followed by the agent's tool log ("Edited a file, read files, ran commands", "Ran bash -n verify/bin/h-032-exit; …") and Codex's closing "Jag fryser bytesen till `773b5081…`". Under the compile's own rule — content pasted from elsewhere inside an owner turn is external evidence, not owner voice — there is no owner use to record; the compile applies that rule correctly elsewhere for the same owner (RND-020: "The owner relayed Codex's R116 contract without own-word adoption"). A targeted scan of every item asserting owner voice against the paste markers in its cited owner turns returns exactly this one unqualified instance, so the class is local, not systematic. It is minor because RND-192 mints no owner authority and the compile's substantive negative-space finding is the opposite and correct (RND-053: every concrete boundary is assistant- or agent-authored) — but as written it tells a reader the owner himself used the PARALLEL_SAFE / SERIAL_TRUST_TRANSITION / DEPENDENCY_BLOCKED vocabulary, which the bound source does not show.
- quote: "Jag rättar nu endast de tre senast bevisade gatebristerna: yttre comprehension-scope, nästlade definitionsnamn och den privata `Path._flavour`-kapabilitetsrelän. Ingen produktkod berörs."

### FIND-046
- finding: RND_OWNER_PROVENANCE_LAUNDERED
- severity: material
- evidence: RND-171 (OWNER_DECISION, prov CONV-001:18-21, CONV-008:179, CONV-025:1-8) claims "The owner replaced his habit of writing 'websearcha, best practices, think hard, iterate' with the assistant's eight-step evidence-driven method … and decided that 'brainstorma' always means the deep variant", with uncertainty "none — verbatim owner decision". Only the second limb is the owner's: his sole words in the span are CONV-025 msg 7, "Det ska alltid vara deep tänker jag". The replacement is the ASSISTANT's offer one turn earlier (CONV-025 msg 6), and the corpus falsifies it as an owner act: the owner keeps writing the phrases in owner turns of at least eight later conversations — including CONV-001 msg 13, five turns before the very range this item cites, in a conversation whose msg 18 ("Vi har ju tidigare ett minne där brainstorma utlöser en optimerad prompt") proves it postdates the CONV-025 memory — and also CONV-005 msg 32, CONV-012 msg 9, CONV-015 msgs 1/188/226, CONV-018 msg 1, CONV-019 msg 1, CONV-022 msg 4, CONV-023 msg 4. An OWNER_DECISION's claim states a behavioural change the owner never decided and the sources contradict, while declaring uncertainty "none".
- quote: "så att du inte längre behöver komma ihåg att skriva `websearcha`, `best practices`, `think hard`, `iterate` varje gång" (CONV-025 msg 6, ChatGPT (assistent)) versus "gå igenom de noggrant, think hard, iterate, websearch. Brainstorma!" (CONV-001 msg 13, Johnny (användare))

### FIND-047
- finding: RND_NEGATIVE_SPACE_OMITTED
- severity: minor
- evidence: CONV-028 msg 5 is an owner turn in which the owner words, in his own voice, a criterion for what matters and two epistemic constraints on the analysis — rank the gaps by how much they slow the project's actual goals rather than by how interesting they are to build; do not agree just to be pleasant; say outright when you lack evidence instead of guessing; end with the three most important decisions, the weakest assumption and the smallest first step. The IR cites that message once (RND-161, prov CONV-028:5-8) and carries from it only "Leta särskilt efter det jag verkar undvika", framing the result as "The assistant's own gap audit, requested by the owner". No item, note or tag anywhere in the IR carries the ranking criterion or the anti-guessing/anti-sycophancy constraints (grep over all 192 claims, uncertainties and the twelve lens notes: zero). This is the corpus's one owner-worded criterion bearing on the question the compile itself names its largest gap — the executive-function lens (NEEDS_RESEARCH) says the owner "adopted en bloc without wording any boundary himself", RND-055 says "no mechanism was owner-worded", and RND-056 presents prioritisation (materiality/urgency/reversibility/capacity) as purely assistant-designed. No claim is literally falsified, because each of those assertions is scoped to attention mechanisms and autonomy boundaries, but a reader of the derived layer cannot see that the owner supplied a prioritisation rule of his own, in the same conversation the Executive Function material comes from.
- quote: "Rangordna luckorna efter hur mycket de bromsar projektets" … "Ge inte medhåll för att vara trevlig." … "säg det rakt ut" (CONV-028 msg 5, Johnny (användare))

### FIND-048
- finding: RND_AUTHORITY_LAUNDERING
- severity: material
- evidence: RND-193 (OBSERVATION, authority_class evidence, sole provenance CONV-028 msg 5, in the executive-function lens basis) claims the owner "wrote in his own voice" the corpus's one criterion for judging what matters, and closes "The criterion is his"; its uncertainty asserts "none that the words are the owner's". CONV-028 msg 5 is external text pasted inside an owner turn. Byte evidence: the turn is 17 non-empty lines wrapped at 59–66 columns with 11 lines ending in a trailing space and 8 three-space hanging indents under a numbered list — a signature present in only 3 of the corpus's 581 owner turns, the other two being an agent stop report (CONV-001 msg 97) and a pasted announcement with the owner's own line appended (CONV-014 msg 1, "Brainstorma är detta nåt användbart för Nortropic"). Every genuinely typed owner turn in CONV-028 is a single unwrapped line (msgs 1, 9, 11, 13, 18, 21, 25, 29, 33, 37, 47, widths 16–354); the thread's only other multi-line owner turns (42, 52, 57) are agent reports and a terminal paste. Msg 5 is 973 characters with zero project nouns and zero typos, in a corpus where the owner's own turns are typo-rich and Nortropic-specific; it says "det här projektet" and never names Nortropic; it is topically disconnected from msgs 1–4 (a Grok Bot brainstorm); and every distinctive phrase in it occurs nowhere else except the assistant's reply. Its only owner-typed contribution is the appended ", brainstorma". The compile states and applies the governing rule elsewhere — RND-192's uncertainty says pasted agent output inside an owner turn is "external evidence and not owner voice", and RND-161 renders the same message safely as "requested by the owner" — so this is the FIND-045 class re-entering the compile through the FIND-047 remediation, and it lands in the one lens whose honest finding is that the owner "adopted en bloc without wording any boundary himself".
- quote: "2. Rangordna luckorna efter hur mycket de bromsar projektets \n   faktiska mål — inte efter hur intressanta de är att bygga." (CONV-028 msg 5, hard-wrapped with trailing space and three-space hanging indent) versus RND-193's "The criterion is his; every mechanism the corpus builds for deciding what work exists is not."

### FIND-049
- finding: RND_OWNER_PROVENANCE_LAUNDERED
- severity: minor
- evidence: RND-190 (OWNER_DECISION, prov CONV-013 143[O], 146[O], 149[A], 150[O], 152[A], 153[A]) opens "Told that the two uploaded Webbförvaltningen masterplan files could be read but not retrieved as bytes, the owner stated the constraint twice in his own words and then authorized a substitute". Both owner turns it counts as statements of the constraint precede the disclosure it places them after: msg 143 is a question ("jag kan inte ladda ner det?") asked before anything was disclosed, msg 144 then told the owner the opposite ("Jag kan göra dem till vanliga `.txt`/`.md`-filer åt dig här så du får klickbara nedladdningar"), msg 146 is a rebuke for not being read, and only msg 149 admits the limit ("referenser/innehåll, inte råa filbytes"). What the owner states twice is his own inability to download, not the system constraint the item attributes to him. The authorization at msg 150 is correctly quoted and correctly placed, and the uncertainty already concedes a neighbouring gap (non-identity stated only at msgs 152–153), so the decision itself stands — but the item is the compile's designated "only owner act that accepts a reconstruction standing in for a lost plan authority" and the framing makes that an informed acceptance the bytes do not show. The compile discloses exactly this modality everywhere else (RND-106 "phrased as a question-pushback", RND-062 "an understanding-check", RND-178 "phrased 'jag tänker'"), so the omission is inconsistent with its own convention.
- quote: "the owner stated the constraint twice in his own words and then authorized a substitute" versus "jag kan inte ladda ner det?" (CONV-013 msg 143) and "Läste du inte vad jag skrev? jag kan inte ladda ner de" (CONV-013 msg 146)

### FIND-050
- finding: RND_SECOND_TRUTH
- severity: minor
- evidence: Three items put the reason the decisive Intake V2 reality test never happened on an owner refusal of an offer that was never made. RND-042 (HYPOTHESIS) ends "a real implement-now pilot was offered and the owner declined to run one"; RND-096 says "the owner declined a real pilot"; RND-097 says "The owner declined any test/pilot of Intake V2". In the cited turn CONV-013 msg 191 the assistant sets the implement-now pilot aside itself, with a future trigger — "Det är inte värt att skapa ett artificiellt projekt bara för att fylla den sista rutan. Nästa gång vi har en **riktig ny Nortropic-idé som vi faktiskt vill bygga**, kör vi: `kör intake — implementera nu`" — and only then does the owner say, at msg 192, "nä, vi kör inget test". He declined a test; he did not decline an available implement-now pilot, because the assistant had already ruled the immediate version out. The substantive facts (the pilot did not happen, INTAKE_V2_FULL_IMPLEMENT_NOW_PILOT stayed NOT_YET_OBSERVED) are correct and correctly labelled; the initiative is reversed. The compile applies the opposite care correctly one item away, at RND-129 ("The assistant withdrew its paid Reality Lane recommendation" / "never explicitly rejected by the owner"), which is what makes this reading a second truth rather than a wording choice.
- quote: "a real implement-now pilot was offered and the owner declined to run one" versus "Det är inte värt att skapa ett artificiellt projekt bara för att fylla den sista rutan."

### FIND-052

- severity: minor

I measured CONV-028 msg 5 byte by byte: 17 non-empty lines, 19 lines counting the two blanks, 22 counting the parser's surrounding newlines — no counting convention I could construct yields 18 (as-parsed 22, `strip()` 19, `rstrip()` 20, `lstrip()` 21, non-empty 17). The other three figures reproduce exactly (11 lines end in a trailing space; 8 lines begin with a three-space hanging indent; max raw width 67, max rstripped 66), and the uniqueness claim reproduces on my own independent scan of all 581 owner turns. The item's whole standing rests on this forensic signature being reproducible by a reader, so a figure that reproduces under no convention is a checkable inaccuracy in the one place the item asks to be checked — though the conclusion it supports is sound.

### FIND-053
- finding: RND_OWNER_PROVENANCE_LAUNDERED
- severity: minor
- evidence: RND-161 (DERIVED_JUDGMENT, sole owner-turn provenance CONV-028 msg 5) opens by quoting the gap-audit prompt as the owner's own request wording, while RND-193 — the item whose whole subject is that same message, and which declares `supports -> RND-161` — establishes at byte level that those words are not shown to be the owner's. Only these two items cite CONV-028 msg 5. I re-measured the turn myself: the quoted fragment sits on the hard-wrapped line "   lagts på annat. Leta särskilt efter det jag verkar undvika " — inside the paste region, carrying both the trailing space and the three-space hanging indent that make up the signature RND-193 relies on — and the owner's only typed contribution to the turn is the appended ", brainstorma". RND-161's uncertainty likewise says "assistant judgement the owner asked for" without noting the request was relayed rather than worded. This is the FIND-045/048 class surviving in the one item the FIND-048 remediation did not touch: the compile now says, one item apart, both that the owner requested the audit in these words and that these words are not shown to be his. The compile applies the correct convention everywhere else (RND-192's uncertainty: pasted agent output inside an owner turn "is external evidence and not owner voice"), which is what makes this a checkable inconsistency rather than a wording choice. Nothing in RND-161's substance is affected — the audit's findings are correctly attributed to the assistant and the item claims no owner authority.
- quote: "The assistant's own gap audit, requested by the owner ('Leta särskilt efter det jag verkar undvika')" (RND-161 claim) versus "explicit — the words are NOT shown to be the owner's" (RND-193 uncertainty)

### FIND-054
- finding: RND_OWNER_PROVENANCE_LAUNDERED
- severity: minor
- evidence: RND-151 (OPTION, identity and authority) names two security constraints as "the only owner-decided pieces", and neither is owner-worded in the cited turns. I read CONV-023 msgs 68-75 in full: the owner's msg 69 is "jag vill köra på extensionen med koppling mot git" and his msg 71 is "vi bygger ingen, det fanns en sa du" — neither mentions tokens; the no-browser-token rule is the assistant's alone, stated at msg 70 ("Då skulle jag **inte** koppla extensionen direkt till GitHub API med en token i browsern") and repeated at msg 72, and it was introduced precisely to redirect the owner's stated want, so "by compatible choice" points the wrong way. The other named piece is no better anchored: RND-151's CONV-020 citations are msgs 73, 78 and 89, all three assistant turns issuing step-by-step instructions, while the owner's one authority-bearing turn in that thread is the uncited msg 57, "jag avinstallerade och installerade plugin igen, fick inte välja något men du har full åtkomst" — a full-access grant, the opposite of least privilege. A reader asking the compile which security constraints the owner actually set is told these two, and the corpus shows neither. Every other "the only owner-…" disclosure I checked holds at byte level (RND-036's CONV-015 msgs 259/263, RND-144's CONV-011 msgs 5/11/15, RND-173's CONV-013 msg 185, RND-167's CONV-008 msg 176 are all genuine owner turns carrying the named content), which is why this one reads as a slip rather than a convention.
- quote: "the only owner-decided pieces are least-privilege choices by execution (CONV-020) and no token in the browser by compatible choice (CONV-023 msg 69/71)" (RND-151 uncertainty) versus "Det enda jag fortfarande inte vill göra är att ge extensionen en GitHub-token i browsern." (CONV-023 msg 72, assistant)

### FIND-055
- finding: RND_SECOND_TRUTH
- severity: minor
- evidence: RND-164 (UNKNOWN) credits the external Codex R116 contract with a three-part stop rule, and its `activation_condition` names the exact message that carries the opposite value. I read both cited turns. The Codex contract relayed in the owner's paste at CONV-016 msg 106 contains one prohibition — "ska R116 stoppa som measurability/authority blocker—ingen approximation" — and closes with `OWNER_DECISION_REQUIRED=NO`, `SELECTED_ARCHITECTURE=OPTION_A_PROVIDER_CONFINEMENT`, `NEXT_ACTION=FRESH_R116_TEST_AUTHOR`. The other two prohibitions and both tokens the activation_condition quotes appear only in the ChatGPT assistant's own prompt at msg 107 ("MEASURABILITY HARD STOP: … DO NOT approximate. DO NOT broaden the sandbox. DO NOT weaken the gate. Stop with: OWNER_DECISION_REQUIRED=YES BLOCKER_CLASS=MEASURABILITY_OR_AUTHORITY"). So assistant-authored elaboration is attributed to an external executor's contract, and a reader who checks the message the activation_condition names finds `OWNER_DECISION_REQUIRED=NO` there instead. The base brief makes `activation_condition` information-only but requires it to be motivated by the source; here it is motivated by msg 107 while citing msg 106. The item's substance — that the Seatbelt question was left open — is true and its UNKNOWN typing is right; what fails is authorship and citation resolvability, the property RND-104 makes a REQUIREMENT of this compile.
- quote: "was left open by Codex's R116 contract, with an explicit stop rule: if not, R116 stops as a measurability/authority blocker — no approximation, no sandbox broadening, no gate weakening" (RND-164 claim) versus "Om macOS Seatbelt inte kan uttrycka den exakta sink-data-behörigheten utan bredare skrivauktoritet ska R116 stoppa som measurability/authority blocker—ingen approximation." followed by "`OWNER_DECISION_REQUIRED=NO`" (CONV-016 msg 106, the whole of the Codex contract on this point)

### FIND-056
- finding: RND_SECOND_TRUTH
- severity: minor
- evidence: RND-088 (OWNER_DECISION, provenance CONV-001 62-70) closes by attributing the v3.0 build to the ChatGPT assistant, which no cited turn supports and which a third actor performed. The labelling half is exact — msg 68 says "Det du precis beslutat är explicit en: > **owner architecture change**" and names the version. The build half is not: msg 70 is the assistant handing the owner a master prompt with "Klistra in detta i en ny Claude Code-session", and the build record is Claude Code's STOP REPORT, pasted by the owner at msg 71 — "STOP REPORT — Nortropic Intake v3.0 (Project Corpus Intake) … Implementations-PR #3 (branch intake-v3: 6867742 impl → fb9538d granskningsfixar → 7614e38 härdningar)" — a message this item does not cite. My scan of all 193 claims finds RND-088 is the only place in the compile where the assistant is said to have built anything; the compile's convention everywhere else keeps author and executor apart, including for this very step (RND-102, on the same v3.0 transition: "Every step was owner-commissioned; every design was assistant-authored") and one item away (RND-177: "Claude Code then reconstructed the R122–R128 product from history"). This is the FIND-051 class in the form the compiler's new two-role checker cannot see, because the actor that is wrong is neither the owner nor the assistant.
- quote: "The assistant labelled it an owner architecture change and built v3.0 Project Corpus Intake." (RND-088 claim) versus "Klistra in detta i en ny Claude Code-session" (CONV-001 msg 70) and the uncited "STOP REPORT — Nortropic Intake v3.0 (Project Corpus Intake)" (CONV-001 msg 71)

### FIND-057
- finding: RND_SECOND_TRUTH
- severity: minor
- evidence: RND-151 uncertainty, written to close FIND-054, dates the least-privilege rule one assistant turn too late and gives it a causal origin the source contradicts. It says the rule was "introduced at CONV-023 msg 70 to redirect the owner's stated want of an extension wired to git (msg 69)". CONV-023 msg 68 — an assistant turn inside the item's own cited range 68-75, i.e. bytes the compiler had — already carries the rule under its own heading, before the owner speaks at msg 69; msg 70 restates it. The item's conclusion (the rule is the assistant's, not the owner's) is correct and is if anything strengthened by msg 68, but a reader tracing the constraint's origin is told it arose as a response to the owner when it was proposed unprompted.
- quote: "### Ännu bättre: extensionen behöver inte få GitHub-token" … "Jag skulle inte stoppa en långlivad GitHub-token i en browser-extension."

### FIND-058
- finding: RND_OWNER_PROVENANCE_LAUNDERED
- severity: minor
- evidence: RND-070 [OWNER_DECISION, uncertainty "none — verbatim owner"] presents as the owner's words a verdict he did not give. Its claim ends "the real-life weaknesses are analog work instructions, medarbetarsamtal and årshjul that are not systems". At CONV-005 msg 25 the owner names the weakness as things being analog rather than systems, exemplified only by daily work tasks with an accompanying work description; medarbetarsamtal, prestationsamtal, förbättringar and årshjul appear in the next sentence as instruments that exist, carrying no verdict. The item moves two of those four into the weakness list, drops the other two, and records no uncertainty — where the same compile hedges far smaller readings elsewhere (RND-157's elliptical fragment, RND-134's hedged rename, RND-051's optative, RND-120's tentative deferral).
- quote: "Tillexempel svagheter i verksamheten jag leder i verkliga livet är att saker är analoga och inte system tex dagliga arbetsuppgifter med tillhörande arbetsbeskrivning (prompt) på hur detta utförs på bästa sätt. En slags arbetsbeskrivning. Vi har medarbetarsamtal, prestationsamtal, förbättringar, ja det finns ett \"årshjul\" som chefer kan arbeta med."

### FIND-059
- finding: RND_NEGATIVE_SPACE_OMITTED
- severity: minor
- evidence: An owner constraint stated in his own words is absent from the IR. At CONV-020 msg 101 the owner requires Pro mode for his innovation work; the assistant ratifies it as design-bearing at msg 102 ("för själva innovationsarbetet ska du absolut fortsätta använda Pro. Det var en viktig distinktion jag borde ha gjort tydligare. Problemet är bara att Pro mode inte får köra Custom Actions"), which is what forced the brain/pen split between Pro and the Custom GPT and set up the transport failure RND-011 records. No item cites CONV-020 msgs 96-104 and no item anywhere carries the constraint. The compile carries this exact class of owner working-mode preference elsewhere as an OWNER_DECISION — RND-148 on model choice and session-versus-API usage from CONV-005 msgs 8-12 — so the omission is below the compile's own threshold rather than a deliberate scope limit.
- quote: "men när jag gör innovation så vill jag ha pro?"

### FIND-060
- finding: RND_AUTHORITY_LAUNDERING
- severity: minor
- evidence: RND-036 (OBSERVATION, cites CONV-015 msgs 273-282 and 285-336) ends its claim "The constitution moved v17→v18 in a separate human HÖGRISK commit." In this corpus "human HÖGRISK" is the named class for a transition a person must author — CONV-015: "It needs a human HÖGRISK commit"; "perform the required human HÖGRISK authorization"; CONV-013 msg 73: "HÖGRISK mänskliga §A6 Foundation CUT-ceremonin". The v18 commit was not one: CONV-015 msg 282 (assistant) "Jag gör nu owner-transitionen i två separata steg", msg 284 "Owner-committen är skapad som separat HÖGRISK commit `cabe6bd4…`", msg 285 "mergat PR #104" and the quoted line below — and no owner turn between msgs 281 and 286 authorizes or reports it (msg 286 is "kan jag köra auto mode?"). The compile knows this pattern and states it one item away: RND-033 "the ChatGPT assistant merged pull requests itself through its own connector and called that an owner-action", with RND-034 typing merge authority UNKNOWN. RND-036's uncertainty ("the only owner verdicts in the chain are CONV-015 msgs 259 and 263") is true but oblique; the claim sentence itself hands a constitutional change the corpus's strongest human-authority label for an act the assistant performed.
- quote: "The constitution moved v17→v18 in a separate human HÖGRISK commit." versus "gjort den separata mänskliga **HÖGRISK `docs/07` v18-committen** och publicerat den via PR #107"

### FIND-061
- finding: RND_OWNER_PROVENANCE_LAUNDERED
- severity: minor
- evidence: RND-108 and RND-109 (both OWNER_DECISION, CONV-001 msgs 85-92 + RQ-024/RQ-025) say only "The owner selected the option; its wording is the assistant's and the sweep's", with uncertainty "selected from a menu rather than written by him". They omit that the ChatGPT assistant told him which option to select in the turn immediately before each submission: msg 88 opens "Välj **1 — Ja, enligt rekommendation**." and msg 90 opens "Välj **1 — Behåll som levererat**." (msg 86 had already pre-announced the direction for groups B, C and D). The compile discloses exactly this for the two sibling items built from the identical speech act — RND-107 "which the assistant then recommended, adopted en bloc" and RND-110 "word for word the text the assistant dictated to the owner ('Skriv detta:')" — so these two fall below the compile's own threshold. It matters because RND-108 is the load-bearing policy item: the rnd-intake lens counts it among the "four review-queue entries the owner answered", and RND-108's own claim is what keeps CONV-028's Grok concepts unpackaged under RQ-027.
- quote: "The owner selected the option; its wording is the assistant's and the sweep's." versus "Välj **1 — Ja, enligt rekommendation**."

### FIND-062
- finding: RND_NEGATIVE_SPACE_OMITTED
- severity: minor
- evidence: RND-163 (UNKNOWN, in the explicit-unknowns-deferred and reality-dogfood bases) declares that the criteria for the Autonomy Kernel v1 checkpoint — "the criteria for 'proven', how many reproducible autonomous closures count" — are "never defined". CONV-017 msg 5, a bound source whose neighbouring turns three other items cite (RND-168 msgs 1-7, RND-169 msgs 4-5, RND-174 msgs 2-5), contains an explicit ten-property operational definition under the heading "När vet vi då att vi inte har byggt ett forskningsprojekt som aldrig blir klart?", including "Agenten kan inte överskrida sin deklarerade blast radius.", "Verifieraren kan verkligen underkänna buildern." and, answering the repeatability question directly, "Vi kan göra samma sak igen med en annan riktig task." — closed by "När det fungerar ska bootstrapen frysas." No item in the IR carries any of it (grep across all 194 claims/uncertainties returns nothing), and RND-027's checkpoint item records only the boundary wording, not proof criteria. The owner-decided gap is real and should stay UNKNOWN, but the corpus does contain a proposed answer and the item tells a reader it does not.
- quote: "and never defined" versus "Den bör istället betyda att följande egenskaper demonstrerats i verklig drift:" … "Vi kan göra samma sak igen med en annan riktig task."

### FIND-063
- finding: RND_NEGATIVE_SPACE_OMITTED
- severity: minor
- evidence: Two owner-voiced operating rules stated in his own words in CONV-008 msg 1 are carried nowhere in the IR, although that turn is cited four times (RND-025 msgs 1-97, RND-028 msgs 1-5, RND-029 msg 1, RND-039 msgs 1-3) and quoted by RND-029 for its executor-handoff sentence only. After a checkout in the shared `~/nortropic/nortropic-system` clone moved HEAD across 51 tracked files over Codex's uncommitted work, the owner states a precedence rule (he will not destroy another agent's untracked work to force his own restore) and a durable isolation constraint (he never touches that clone again; all his work goes through `worktrees/`). Both are exactly the class the compile carries elsewhere as owner-voiced constraint — RND-180 records "jag arbetar ju i codex och vi vill ha flera pararella agenter igång" as an owner-constraint OBSERVATION — and both bear directly on the corpus's own shared-clone and cwd hazard family (RND-039, RND-021). This is the same shape as the omission accepted as FIND-059 last round.
- quote: "jag raderar inte deras arbete för att tvinga igenom det" / "Jag rör inte den klonen igen; allt mitt arbete går via worktrees under worktrees/."

- finding: RND_NEGATIVE_SPACE_OMITTED
- severity: minor
- evidence: The compile's own corpus records a second known source gap that the IR's negative-space layer does not carry. review-queue.md RQ-029 (and its closure RQ-030) state that 19 of the 30 bound sources were NOT re-captured at the 2026-09-01 incremental sweep and were judged unchanged from the platform's `update_time` field rather than from bytes; project-manifest.json confirms it — CONV-004, CONV-005, CONV-008 through CONV-027 (except the r2 recaptures) carry `captured_at: 2026-08-31` while inventory_revision 38 is dated 2026-09-01. Consequence: any owner turn added to those conversations after 2026-08-31 is absent from this compile by construction. The IR carries the sibling sweep gap — inventoried-but-uncaptured attachments (RQ-022/RQ-026) — explicitly as RND-111 ("Their contribution to this compile is unknown by construction"), but nothing in the 195 items, the source_set, or the explicit-unknowns-deferred lens note mentions the currency gap; `grep` for RQ-029, RQ-030 or `update_time` in rnd-ir.json returns nothing, and the lens note claims "no consolidated register existed before this compile". A reader of the IR alone therefore cannot learn that two thirds of the bound sources rest on platform metadata for being current.
- quote: "update_time är plattformsmetadata, inte byte-bevis; deras r1-bytes står orörda och hash-verifierade, men \"oförändrad sedan 2026-08-31\" vilar för dessa 19 på plattformens egen deklaration."

### FIND-065
- finding: RND_OWNER_PROVENANCE_LAUNDERED
- severity: minor
- evidence: RND-196 (UNKNOWN, provenance RQ-029 and RQ-030 only, in the explicit-unknowns-deferred basis) closes by telling a reader that the currency gap it has just disclosed is already scheduled for closure by an owner-confirmed plan. No owner turn says so. The plan is CONV-001 msg 116, which RQ-029 and RQ-030 both name as the basis ("CONV-001 r2 msg 115–116"); its final-intake step reads "+ new chats + daily reports + load-bearing attachments" and nothing else, and RND-091 — the compile's own OWNER_DECISION for that adoption — records exactly those three additions and no re-capture. The assistant turn the owner adopts, msg 115, defines "incremental" the opposite way ("Intake behöver då inte läsa om de gamla 27 från noll", with the diagram line "oförändrad chat → NO-OP / återanvänd"), which is the mechanism that produces the gap rather than its remedy; neither "återfång" nor "byte-verif" occurs anywhere in CONV-001, at either revision. The wording also hardens across three steps that the compile flattens: RQ-029 recommends ("bör samtliga källor återfångas eller byte-verifieras … i linje med den ägarbekräftade … planen"), RQ-030 asserts ("ingår redan i"), and RND-196 states it as owner-confirmed fact with the source's "eller" turned into "and". Everything else in the item is correct and I verified it independently — the nineteen-of-thirty arithmetic against enumeration-evidence-r30.json, the RQ-030 quote, and the currency-versus-integrity distinction. What fails is the one clause that tells a reader the gap needs no owner attention, in the item created to bring that gap to an owner's attention; the compile keeps author and adopter apart everywhere else, including for this very turn at RND-091.
- quote: "Full re-capture and byte verification before a Recompile source cut is already part of the owner-confirmed final incremental intake plan." (RND-196 claim) versus "FINAL INCREMENTAL IMPROVEMENTS INTAKE\n+ new chats\n+ daily reports\n+ load-bearing attachments" (CONV-001 msg 116, the owner's own turn) and "Intake behöver då inte läsa om de gamla 27 från noll" (CONV-001 msg 115)

### FIND-066
- finding: RND_NEGATIVE_SPACE_OMITTED
- severity: minor
- evidence: RQ-027 (OPEN, `owner_judgment_required: yes`) names four assistant-synthesised concepts nominated "till Recompile" and recommends they "förbli råevidens för RND_COMPILE" — this compile. Two survive: Role Shell at RND-057/RND-075 and Quiet Organization Contract at RND-055 (quote "Healthy autonomous work should be quiet."). Attention Compiler and Experience-to-Capability Compiler are absent by name and by substance — my scans over all 196 claims, quotes, uncertainties and tags return zero hits for "Attention Compiler", "divergen", "stated priorit", "observed activity/attention", "Experience-to-Capability", "Capability Candidate", "trajector", "demonstrat" (in this sense), "teach" and "Watch Me Work". The omission is not inattention: CONV-028 msg 3 is cited by RND-055/075/113, msg 24 is the single most-cited message in the compile (16 items) and msg 32 is cited by four more, and all three name the Attention Compiler — the source heads it "Ännu större guldkorn" and calls it a better primitive than the Owner Attention Queue, while RND-055 takes only the smaller "quiet agents" idea from the same message. The nearest carried fragments are not the concepts: RND-055's mechanisms are all about *when to interrupt the owner*, not measuring declared priorities against observed attention, and RND-142's Qualification Ladder qualifies an already-defined capability rather than deriving one from an observed trajectory. This lands in executive-function, the lens the compile itself calls "the largest unexplored gap", and in owner-attention, its densest coverage-check tag. Nothing in the negative-space layer records the drop, while RND-108 tells a reader "The same policy kept CONV-028's four Grok-derived concepts unpackaged (RQ-027, still open)" — inviting the reading that all four survive here as evidence. The owner's own completeness instruction for that thread (CONV-028 msg 21) is cited by no item.
- quote: "fyra assistentsyntetiserade koncept nominerade \"till Recompile\" (Attention Compiler, Role Shell, Experience-to-Capability Compiler, Quiet Organization Contract)" (RQ-027) versus "Och **Attention Compiler + Role Shell + Experience-to-Capability Compiler + Quiet Organization Contract** tycker jag är fyra genuina nya guldkorn som kom ur just den här genomgången." (CONV-028 msg 3) and the owner at msg 21: "ja, vi behöver gå på djupet, förstå, validera och ta med oss allt \"guld\" och guldkorn och lärdomar, fortsätt brainstorma"

### FIND-067
- finding: RND_OWNER_PROVENANCE_LAUNDERED
- severity: minor
- evidence: The compile grades the same evidence shape — a one-line owner "ja" over a very long assistant design message — inconsistently, and the sharpest case is two items on the identical turn. RND-100 (OWNER_DECISION) and RND-101 (OPTION) both rest on CONV-029 msg 11, a 129-character owner turn approving msg 10, a 29,806-character assistant Function Intake design. RND-101's uncertainty reads "medium — assistant design, owner blanket-approved (msg 11)"; RND-100's reads "none for the adoptions". RND-157 flags the same shape at CONV-012 msg 4 (21 characters over a 16,810-character message) as "medium on granularity — blanket approval of a long assistant message", so the hedge is the compile's own convention. RND-095 departs from it too: CONV-010 msg 6 is 43 characters approving an 18,143-character message, uncertainty "none for the adoption". I measured every length myself. The claims are not false — RND-100 quotes 'detta är bra' and names the content assistant-authored — but recording "none" for the adoption of a 30,000-character design the owner never re-worded overstates how much owner backing survives, in the one field the contract reserves for saying so, and contradicts the sibling item citing the same turn.
- quote: "none for the adoptions; the Function Intake content (13 passes, autonomy envelope, Attention Contract, FunctionEval) is assistant-authored" (RND-100 uncertainty) versus "medium — assistant design, owner blanket-approved (msg 11)" (RND-101 uncertainty, same turn) over the owner's whole turn: "Ja, detta är bra. Jag har en kompis Viktor som driver företag som var nyfiken på det autonoma som kan vara en test kandidat också"

### FIND-068
- finding: RND_SECOND_TRUTH
- severity: minor
- evidence: RND-092's uncertainty says of CONV-005 msg 60 that "the three 'owner decisions' in msg 60 (length exception, related links, commit ceremony) appear only as execution evidence, never owner words". I read msg 60: it is a `ChatGPT (assistent)` turn in which the assistant makes those three decisions itself — it opens "Jag skulle fatta de tre owner-besluten så här:", numbers them ("Godkänn en kontrollerad längdexception", "Godkänn de fyra ömsesidiga `related:`-länkarna", "Godkänn commit ceremony för båda repona"), then says "Ge Claude detta:" and emits a text block headed "Owner decisions approved." for the owner to relay. The execution evidence is the *next* turn, owner msg 61, a pasted Claude report opening "Both commits are in." So the three decisions do not appear "only as execution evidence" — they appear first as an assistant-authored approval draft, which is the strongest instance in this thread of the pattern the compile names precisely elsewhere (RND-073: "The gate text labelled 'OWNER DECISION' is assistant-authored"). The conclusion "never owner words" is correct and nothing is laundered toward the owner; what fails is that the item misdescribes its own cited message and loses the instance, understating assistant authorship in a compile whose discipline is exactly who said what.
- quote: "the three 'owner decisions' in msg 60 (length exception, related links, commit ceremony) appear only as execution evidence, never owner words" (RND-092 uncertainty) versus CONV-005 msg 60, a ChatGPT turn: "Jag skulle fatta de tre owner-besluten så här:" … "Ge Claude detta:" … "Owner decisions approved."

Codex vet jag har hållt på mycket med h032?"; a constraint: "jag vill bara ha det som löser framtida onödiga ägarstopp"; a specific instruction: "Vi har codex usage igen som vi använder senare när claude är klar") rather than adopting the long message's content, and the two my own detector flagged beyond the compiler's list (RND-130, RND-175) disclose the granularity in substance ("the owner does not restate the plan's content"; "the mechanism is assistant-designed"). **FIND-068: REMEDIATED** — RND-092's uncertainty now reads "the three 'owner decisions' (length exception, related links, commit ceremony) are never owner words: at msg 60 the assistant takes them itself and drafts the approval text for the owner to relay, and msg 61 is the pasted execution report that followed", and I verified CONV-005 msg 60 is a `ChatGPT (assistent)` turn containing "Jag skulle fatta de tre owner-besluten så här:", "Ge Claude detta:" and "Owner decisions approved.", and that msg 61 is an owner turn opening "Both commits are in." Semantically I read all 197 claims and all twelve lens notes against their bases, and used 6 sub-agents to read every one of the 581 owner turns of all 30 sources (111 of which no item cites), re-verifying every nomination at byte level myself. Falsification attempts that failed in the compile's favour: that RND-157 asserts an absence its own citation contains, since CONV-012 msg 1 ends "Vi implementerar INTE Aquarium ännu." — my own wrap-signature test settles it for the compile, that turn being 240 lines with a maximum line length of 75 and zero lines over 90 columns, a hard wrap present in no other owner turn in the corpus, exactly as the item's uncertainty says ("CONV-012 msg 1's body is the assistant-authored handoff prompt pasted by the owner"); that RND-105/RND-106 slide "Det är väl inte ett års av arkitekturprojekt" from Webbförvaltningen onto Recompile (CONV-028 msg 12 resolves the referent itself: "## Och nej — absolut inte ett års arkitekturprojekt … Post-Bootstrap Recompile ska vara en kompilering och rebaseline"); that RND-181's "The owner ran the remediation" lacks owner voice (CONV-016 msgs 47 and 53 are owner turns carrying the pasted reports, and the item is an OBSERVATION, not an owner decision); that RND-195 overstates the isolation rule (the later "REPOSITORY = /Users/…/nortropic-system" lines are pasted publication-bundle identity, not the owner's cwd, and the item already says the rule's persistence is unevidenced); that RND-123's "twenty-two instantiations across seven conversations" miscounts its eight cited sources (CONV-005's single entry there, msg 28, is an owner turn cited for backing, exactly as RND-053 handles CONV-008 msg 96); CONV-002 and CONV-004 are confirmed to contain zero owner turns, as RND-113 states.

### FIND-069
- finding: RND_SECOND_TRUTH
- severity: material
- evidence: RND-197, the OPTION added to close FIND-066, states as the assistant's later argument something the cited turns do not contain, and anchors it with a quote taken from a different argument. Its claim says the assistant "later argues that a vent signal … makes the compiler far better defined because the input becomes reported friction rather than inferred activity", and its `quote` is "Attention Compiler blir därmed mycket bättre definierad". I located that string: it occurs exactly once in CONV-028, as the section heading in msg 24, where "därmed" refers to the immediately preceding mission-command/OCIR section ending "**Attention should be decision-linked, not event-linked.** Det tycker jag är nytt och starkt." Msg 24's stated reason is the opposite of the item's: it *weakens* the compiler's original input — "Efter mission-command-linsen skulle jag **försvaga dess authority rejält**. Observed attention är sensorinformation. Inte strategi." — and replaces the msg-3 input set with OWNER INTENT / GOALS / OCIR – DECISION NEEDS / ORGANIZATION STATE + OBSERVED ATTENTION. The vent signal appears only in msg 32, under the heading "# Lovables Vent Tool ger dessutom stöd åt Attention Compiler" — *additionally supports*, not "far better defined" — and its routing there is friction → pattern → toil/capability gap candidate → Executive Function, never into the compiler's inputs. The stated cause is absent from the source: "bättre definierad" occurs once in the whole conversation (msg 24), and no equivalent of "reported friction rather than inferred activity" occurs anywhere. The compile therefore presents the Attention Compiler in its msg-3 form ("takes stated priorities, observed activity and organisation state") as having been sharpened by a mechanism that did not sharpen it, while the source's own revision of that concept — the demotion of observed attention to sensor information — is carried by no item; the nearest fragment, RND-055's "decision-linked not event-linked attention", records the law but not that it re-founded this concept. A reader sent to CONV-028 msgs 24 and 32 by this item's provenance would find a materially different mechanism from the one the claim describes, in the lens the compile itself calls its largest gap.
- quote: "and later argues that a vent signal, where an agent reports on its own when a tool or an instruction is failing it, makes the compiler far better defined because the input becomes reported friction rather than inferred activity" (RND-197 claim) versus CONV-028 msg 24: "# Attention Compiler blir därmed mycket bättre definierad" … "Efter mission-command-linsen skulle jag **försvaga dess authority rejält**. Observed attention är sensorinformation. Inte strategi." and CONV-028 msg 32: "# Lovables Vent Tool ger dessutom stöd åt Attention Compiler"

### FIND-070
- finding: RND_OWNER_PROVENANCE_LAUNDERED
- severity: material
- evidence: RND-195 is typed OWNER_DECISION with authority_class `owner` and claims the owner "stated two rules in his own words", at maximal confidence ("none that both rules are the owner's own words"). Its sole provenance is CONV-008 msg 1, and both rules sit inside a block the owner introduces, in his own voice, as a report of something that happened elsewhere: line 7 of that turn is the bolded header "**En incident du behöver känna till som hände i en annan terminal med Claude:**", after which line 8 begins with a leading space and the block runs in an agent's forensic register (exact SHAs c00d5540 / c9275554, "51 spårade filer", "verifierat"). Three independent tests agree the first person in that block is the other Claude session, not the owner. (1) The block contains the markdown escape `\~/nortropic/nortropic-system`; my own signature test over the whole corpus finds backslash-escapes in 17 of 581 owner turns and 1 of 1139 assistant turns, i.e. they mark pasted tool output inside owner turns, and CONV-008 msg 1's only such escape is inside this block while the owner's own lines 1 and 5 carry none. (2) The block says "jag raderar inte **deras** arbete" and "när **de** bestämt vad som ska hända", where "de" is Codex — the register of a third agent reporting on a second. (3) Decisively, the in-thread recipient reads it that way: CONV-008 msg 5 says "`main @ c9275554` som **Claude** checkade ut", "A different Claude terminal accidentally disturbed the shared working directory", and "While trying to read canonical main, Claude ran: `git checkout main` in that shared dirty clone." The compile applies exactly this rule elsewhere (RND-192's uncertainty: "CONV-015 msg 19 is pasted agent output inside an owner turn, which under this compile's own rule is external evidence and not owner voice"), and it already takes the genuine owner voice out of this same message at RND-029 ("ämnar att claude code får fortsätta då codex inte har usaege just nu", line 1, before the paste). So one of the 77 OWNER_DECISIONs converts a pasted agent's operating discipline into an owner constraint, and the claim's second half — "The isolation rule is a durable constraint on where he operates" — is a rule the owner never spoke.
- quote: "**En incident du behöver känna till som hände i en annan terminal med Claude:**" … "Jag rör inte den klonen igen; allt mitt arbete går via worktrees under worktrees/." (CONV-008 msg 1) versus CONV-008 msg 5: "While trying to read canonical main, Claude ran:" … "A different Claude terminal accidentally disturbed the shared working directory" — against RND-195's claim "the owner stated two rules in his own words" and its uncertainty "none that both rules are the owner's own words"

### FIND-071
- finding: RND_SECOND_TRUTH
- severity: minor
- evidence: RND-197's rewritten vent-signal clause still describes the Lovable vent tool as reporting that "a tool or instruction is failing it". The source's second term is the environment, not an instruction. CONV-028 msg 32 says "Lovable låter agenten själv rapportera när dess verktyg eller **miljö** hindrar arbetet", its worker example is "Capability X hindrar mig", and msg 32's earlier mention calls it "en 'vent tool' där agenten själv kan rapportera friktion den inte kan lösa". The word "instruktion" occurs nowhere in the vent passage, and the vent tool is mentioned in only two places in the entire bound corpus, both in this one message. "instruction" is inherited wording from the version FIND-069 falsified, and a reader sent to msg 32 by this item's provenance would find a differently scoped signal from the one the claim names.
- quote: "A vent signal, where an agent reports that a tool or instruction is failing it" (RND-197 claim) versus CONV-028 msg 32: "Lovable låter agenten själv rapportera när dess verktyg eller miljö hindrar arbetet"
